| D24 | Dose model (Sean's proportional rule). Accepted: proportional credit against an applicable benchmark, hidden amounts 0, BCAA/EAA once as a set, genuinely unassessable stays so. Rejected: the 25%-of-heaviest mass demotion. Three choices: (1) weighting by purpose and identity, and whether a non-purpose ingredient (HMB's calcium) adds to Dose; (2) whether an incomplete Dose assessment carries an ordinary numeric total and tier, then A/B/C/R; (3) excess: the accepted September30 master plan permits consideration of distinct Dose appropriateness and Safety risk consequences; exact magnitudes, exposure basis and overlap treatment remain Sean decisions. This supersedes the older Safety-only preference referenced in the historical draft audit below. The codex/dose-calibration lane's draft test encodes E1 and R before these are decided. **Audit 2026-09-30 of that lane's uncommitted draft (`scoring_v4/modules/proportional_dose.py`, not committed, base c581a9ae, 59 commits behind main):** not landable. It follows two accepted rules (hidden amounts 0; BCAA/EAA once) but breaks the rest: an unbenchmarked main ingredient counts as 0 credit instead of staying unassessable (Lecithin 500 13260: Dose 16 -> 0/25); it re-implements the rejected 25%-of-heaviest demotion; excess uses 0.75/0.5 Dose caps and pre-records B7 so B7 no longer deducts Dose (choice 3 says Safety); 70/30 weighting pre-decides choice 1; benchmarks (melatonin 0.3 mg, creatine 3 g, EPA+DHA 2 g, fiber 7 g, BCAA 5 g, EAA 8 g) and caps are code literals, and it copies `dose_subscale.archetype_reference`; it overwrites every non-probiotic route's Dose after the route module ran (a second Dose engine at the seam). Frozen replay (1,261 labels, `~/pg_quality/dosecal_audit_20260930/`): 1,198 move, Dose down 1,091 / up 107, generic mean -9.8 (worst -17), 0 status/route changes. the generic alias "protein" (removed in e769134a). The record's current aliases cover whey, casein, milk,
